local function collectText(obj)
    if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox") then
        if obj.Text and obj.Text ~= "" and not table.find(texts, obj.Text) then
            table.insert(texts, obj.Text)
        end
    end
    if obj.Name and obj.Name ~= "" and not table.find(texts, obj.Name) then
        table.insert(texts, obj.Name)
    end
end

for _, v in pairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
    collectText(v)
end

local result = table.concat(texts, ",")
